Review the answer’s target

Condition names and repeat numbers are hidden. Read the question and decide whether the answer concerns the original artifact. A probability change is not itself target drift. Do not treat agent statements as audited execution facts.

Mixed means the answer combines original and replacement artifacts as if they answered the same question. Unjudgeable stays outside the scored denominator; coverage is reported. Cite text in the notes for off-target or mixed labels.
